## Spot-check notes

- `peaso/checks/azure_extended.py:165`: SQL TDE reads `properties.state` for user databases; it does not read backup settings or Managed Instances.
- `peaso/checks/claude_azure_network.py:1010`: Network Watcher flow logs are not VNet diagnostic logs.
- `peaso/checks/claude_azure_identity.py:758`: classic Front Door evaluator succeeds if **any** frontend endpoint has a WAF link.
- `peaso/checks/claude_azure_network.py:870`: NSG association evaluator reads subnets only, omitting NIC links.
- `peaso/checks/azure_ports_services.py:185,192`: Search public-network and private-link checks were available but missed.

## Doubts

- Several translated source controls use Azure names for AWS-only constructs. Proposed checks remain research specifications; their API shape and field availability require implementation review.
- Read-only GET/List sources do not prove that policy, audit, or backup behavior operated at runtime. Proposed checks remain not assessed until implemented and tested.

## Final specification corrections

- **pci_4.0_azure / 10.3.3.20**: NEW_CHECK checks=[] proposals=['azure_recovery_vault_10_3_3_20_803bff6'] → NOT_APPLICABLE checks=[] proposals=[]. Azure SQL Database PaaS automated backups are governed by retention policy, not Azure Backup plans with per-database membership.
- **pci_4.0_azure / 10.3.3.21**: NEW_CHECK checks=[] proposals=['azure_recovery_vault_10_3_3_21_4e1f30a'] → NOT_APPLICABLE checks=[] proposals=[]. Azure SQL Managed Instance automated backups are service-managed; Azure Backup plan membership is not a Managed Instance control.
- **pci_4.0_azure / 3.5.1.22**: NEW_CHECK checks=[] proposals=['azure_cosmos_account_storage_infrastructure_encryption_is_enabled_4935121'] → NOT_APPLICABLE checks=[] proposals=[]. Azure Cosmos DB and Azure SQL Database storage encryption is service-managed; neither has the user-toggle assumed by the source rule.
- **pci_4.0_azure / 8.3.2.37**: NEW_CHECK checks=[] proposals=['azure_cosmos_account_storage_infrastructure_encryption_is_enabled_4935121'] → NOT_APPLICABLE checks=[] proposals=[]. Azure Cosmos DB and Azure SQL Database storage encryption is service-managed; neither has the user-toggle assumed by the source rule.
- **pci_4.0_azure / 3.5.1.8**: NEW_CHECK checks=[] proposals=['azure_storage_account_3_5_1_8_535d488'] → NOT_APPLICABLE checks=[] proposals=[]. Azure DevOps pipeline logs have no per-pipeline storage encryption toggle; Azure Storage service-side encryption is managed on the storage account.
- **pci_4.0_azure / 8.3.2.12**: NEW_CHECK checks=[] proposals=['azure_storage_account_3_5_1_8_535d488'] → NOT_APPLICABLE checks=[] proposals=[]. Azure DevOps pipeline logs have no per-pipeline storage encryption toggle; Azure Storage service-side encryption is managed on the storage account.

## Final validation

- Unique requirement rows corrected: 1378.
- Summary: `{"manual": 9, "mapped_verified": 54, "mapping_fixed": 68, "new_check": 1067, "not_applicable": 279, "partial": 228, "requirements_total": 1705}`.
- Validation: `PYTHONPATH=. nice -n 15 python docs/compliance-ledgers/.work/azure-pci/validate.py` passed; 1,705 exact requirement IDs and 200 distinct proposed-check specs.
- Remaining doubt: Azure-translated controls and some compound API predicates require implementation-time field verification; no proposed check is an implemented native check.

## Repair pass

- Reviewed both frameworks, all 1705 input requirements, 46 distinct mapped requirement/evaluator combinations, manual and out-of-scope rows, and every surviving proposed check.
- Applied the spot-check correction for `pci_4.0_azure / 1.5.1.40`: the proposed NSG predicate tests the supplied authorized TCP/UDP port set and uses one neutral ID across the repeated requirement.
- Downgraded native mappings that covered only a subset, removed proposals for Azure-inapplicable source concepts, and made manual reasons identify the evidence to review.
- Proposed checks now name the resource and API or collected source, exact fields, and a failure predicate; same-control duplicate proposals were merged.
- Decision-count changes versus the pre-repair ledger: `{"manual": 52, "mapped_verified": -3, "mapping_fixed": -9, "new_check": -236, "not_applicable": 189, "partial": 7}`.
- Final decision counts: `{"manual": 61, "mapped_verified": 51, "mapping_fixed": 59, "new_check": 831, "not_applicable": 468, "partial": 235, "requirements_total": 1705}`.
- Validation: `PYTHONPATH=. python3 docs/compliance-ledgers/.work/azure-pci/validate.py` passed; all 1,705 requirement IDs appear exactly once, every native ID is in the frozen check index, 144 distinct proposal bodies are internally consistent, and the todo checklist matches the ledger.
- Azure API cross-checks: [Key Vault key rotation policy](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/key-vault/keys/how-to-configure-key-rotation) supports the key-versus-secret distinction; [VNet flow logs](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/network-watcher/vnet-flow-logs-overview) use VNet target resource IDs; [ACR scanning](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/container-registry/scan-images-defender) is configured through Defender at subscription scope; [Front Door WAF](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/web-application-firewall/afds/waf-front-door-policy-settings) supports policy association.
